| ENGINE-E1 / PROTOCOL-CORE | E1-PROTOCOL-CORE-CUTOVER-GREEN | e993dfc6cbd0d1688ef67f153cb1164a0e144955 + working tree | Cut over `protocol/protocol.cpp` to the Rust `fcitx5-protocol-core` codec through a thin C ABI bridge. New `protocol/protocol_ffi.h` declares the flat `Fcitx*C` structures and 23 exported functions (11 typed encode + 11 typed decode + `decode_header`) added in `rust/protocol-core/src/capi.rs` with panic containment; string fields flow through caller-provided arenas with a stack-then-heap probe so typical frames cost a single call. `protocol.cpp` now only marshals between the unchanged `protocol.h` DTOs and the C structures; the old C++ `Writer`/`Reader`, metadata/payload validation, `decodeHeader`/`decodeFrame`/encode/decode internals were deleted, so Rust is the single authoritative codec and `protocol.h` API plus all call sites (engine/ipc/launcher/ui/control/tests) are unchanged. | ENGINE-E4 / IPC-SCOPE | E4-3-PER-CONNECTION-SESSION-RUST-GREEN | f958fb9 + working tree | E4-3: the engine server's per-connection session state moved to Rust. C ABI in `engine_core_ffi.h`/`capi.rs`: `session_create`/`session_destroy`/`session_begin_hello`/`session_accept_frame`/`session_complete_request` with panic containment and null fail-closed. `src/engine/fcitx_engine_main.cpp`: `handleRequest` validates every frame through the Rust session (hello via `session_begin_hello`, all other frames via `session_accept_frame`, successful requests recorded via `session_complete_request`); `serve` creates one Rust session per connection; the C++ `handshakeComplete`/`lastRequestId` locals and the direct `validate_engine_epoch`/`accept_frame_sequence` calls are deleted. Source contract now requires the five session ABI calls, forbids `bool handshakeComplete`/`std::uint64_t lastRequestId`/`GetSystemTimeAsFileTime`/the old ordering comparison and the two direct validators in `fcitx_engine_main.cpp`. | TSF-REGISTER / REGISTRATION | TSF-REGISTER-PATH-BUGFIX-GREEN | e5c181a | Bugfix: `rust/tsf-poc/src/lib.rs` `current_module_path()` used `GetModuleFileNameW(None)`, which names the current process executable. When the elevated register helper (`fcitx5-register.exe`) loads the TSF DLL and calls `DllRegisterServer`, the CLSID `InprocServer32` default was written as the register helper's own path, so `--status` reported `path_mismatch` forever and `Start Fcitx5.exe` failed with "Administrator approval is required once to register the TSF components" even when elevated. Fix: resolve this module's own handle via `GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS, current_module_path)` (unchanged-refcount) before `GetModuleFileNameW`, so `InprocServer32` and `RegisterProfile` record the real `fcitx5-tsf.dll` path. Passed: `fcitx5-tsf-poc` 27 cargo tests; clippy `-D warnings`; fmt; rebuilt x64/x86 shipping `fcitx5-tsf.dll`; elevated `register --repair` exit 0 for both architectures; `--status` now reports `registered` and the registry `InprocServer32` default points at the packaged `tsf\x64\fcitx5-tsf.dll`; re-staged package artifacts contain the fixed DLL (hash-verified). | The TSF registration path bug (introduced by the R3-03 Rust TSF cutover) is fixed and the live registry on this machine was repaired. `RUST-R3-TSF-POC` remains manual-pending for real host matrix evidence. |
